**Input**

**Tool `writeBrain`**

```json
{
  "text": "1. Before every move: check all checks, captures, threats for both sides.\n2. Never leave pieces hanging; count attackers vs defenders before trading.\n3. Opening: control center (e4/d4 or e5/d5), develop knights before bishops, castle early.\n4. Don't move the queen early or the same piece twice without reason.\n5. Keep king safe; avoid pawn moves in front of castled king.\n6. When ahead in material, trade pieces and simplify; when behind, keep complexity.\n7. Rooks to open files, knights to outposts, bishops on long diagonals.\n8. Endgame: activate king, push passed pawns, rooks behind passers.\n9. Always verify a move is legal; if refused, recheck the board.\n10. Resign only when clearly lost; otherwise fight for draws."
}
```

## Minute 0, pass 2

**Input**

**Unlocked tools:** viewBoard, readBrain

**Text**

I've saved my first strategy brain (version 1, 118 words, under the 150-word limit). These are the rules I'll play by:

1. Before every move, check all checks, captures and threats for both sides.
2. Never leave a piece undefended, and count attackers against defenders before trading.
3. In the opening, take the centre, develop knights before bishops and castle early.
4. Don't bring the queen out early or move the same piece twice without a reason.
5. Keep my king safe and avoid pawn moves in front of it after castling.
6. Trade down when I'm ahead in material; keep the position complicated when I'm behind.
7. Put rooks on open files, knights on outposts and bishops on long diagonals.
8. In the endgame, bring the king in, push passed pawns and put rooks behind them.
9. If a move is refused as illegal, look at the board again.
10. Resign only in a clearly lost position; otherwise play for a draw.

Right now only viewBoard and readBrain are open, so I'll wait for game 1 to start before making a move.

I updated the strategy brain (now version 4, 135 words). What this game changed:
- **Rule 3:** Don't play Nf5 when recapturing exf5 takes away the pawn that defends d5. That's how I lost a pawn on moves 21–22.
- **Rule 7:** Any final capture has to happen by my own move 40, so set it up by move 37. Late on, I had no winning capture left in reach.
- **Rule 9:** Before trading off the last rooks, look for loose pawns the enemy queen can pick up with checks. My b2 pawn fell to Qc1+ and Qxb2.
- **Rule 2:** When counting attackers and defenders, include pieces lined up behind others. On d6 I nearly missed that Black's second rook added a third attacker.

The other rules are unchanged. Game 4, where I play Black, comes next.
